# English translation: to do

Generated by comparing this book with the Latin book and with Divinum Officium's English
(github.com/DivinumOfficium/divinum-officium, `web/www/horas/English`). Divinum Officium credits
the Marquess of Bute's translation (1908) for most of its breviary English, and its collects,
lessons, responsories, antiphons and versicles generally agree with the existing English here.
Texts were imported from it only when its Latin matched this book's Latin closely and nothing
marked its English as another translation. Everything that could not be filled that way is
listed below, by Latin file, as `tags` — `key`.

Divinum Officium occasionally substitutes another translation (usually Douay) for a single
text without any sign of it, so imported scriptural antiphons and responsories may need
checking against Bute; e.g. the Pentecost antiphon "The charity of God * is poured forth in our
hearts…" is Douay. Imported texts keep Divinum Officium's spelling (e.g. Caesar, offense).

## 1. Missing: Divinum Officium's English is not Bute

Divinum Officium has these texts, but in another translation, so they need Bute's text from another source.

## 4. Imported lesson headings from Divinum Officium's editing (439)

Divinum Officium rewords lesson headings ("From the Sermons of…", "(of Hippo.)"). Where the existing English already translated the same Latin heading, that translation was used instead; these were not.

- `commune-sanctorum/bmv-sabbatum.json`
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `januarius`: "From the letter of Blessed Ambrose, bishop of Milan, to Pope Siricius"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `februarius`: "From the book of St. Jerome, Priest, against Jovian"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `martius`: "From the book of St. Irenaeus, Bishop and Martyr, Against Heretics"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `majus`: "From the Treatise of St. Augustine, Bishop, on the Creed, to the Catechumens"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `junius`: "Sermon of St. Bernard, Abbot"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `julius`: "From the letter of Blessed Ambrose, Bishop to Pope Siricius"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `augustus`: "From the Commentary of Pope St. Gregory on the books of Kings"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `september`: "From the epistle of Pope St. Leo to Pulcheria Augusta"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `october`: "A sermon of St. Bernard, Abbot"
  - `bmv-sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-iii` — `november`: "From the Commentary of St. Basil, Bishop, on the Prophet Isaias"

## 6. Possible transcription errors in the existing English

Left unchanged because the right reading is uncertain.

- `tagged/commune-sanctorum/martyres.json`: "they have no strength to withstand the Almight of God" (Almightiness? Might?)
- `tagged/proprium-sanctorum/augustus/joachim.json`: "praise-worthy" (a line-break hyphen kept?)
- `tagged/proprium-de-tempore/post-pentecosten/hebdomada-xx-pentecostes.json`: "His was therefore a grievously imperfect faith" (Divinum Officium: "This was…")
- `tagged/commune-sanctorum/confessor.json`: "And Who is his strength?" (Divinum Officium: "in his strength")
- `tagged/commune-sanctorum/vidua.json`: "and bought this field" (Divinum Officium: "that field")
- `untagged/psalmi/psalmus-cxviii.txt`: "they are a joy to my heart" (Douay: "the joy of my heart")
- Spaces before `;` and `:` from the printed source remain in about 18 places (e.g. `commune-sanctorum/martyr.json`, "the lying-in-wait of the Jews : how I kept back").
## 7. Existing English entries whose tags match no Latin entry

These were never used, since translations are matched to the Latin by tags; most are likely tagging slips.

- `tagged/proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/hebdomada-i-adventus.json`: Thursday and Friday Matins responsories (`feria-v`/`feria-vi`, `responsorium-i`/`responsorium-iii`) don't match the Latin's numbering
- `tagged/commune-sanctorum/confessor-pontifex.json`: `nocturna-i` `lectio-i`
- `tagged/commune-sanctorum/doctor.json`: `nocturna-iii` `lectio-i`
- `tagged/votiva/pro-sanctis.json`: `versiculus`, `collecta`
- `tagged/psalterium/matutinum.json`: `invitatorium`; Paschal-time ferial Matins antiphons (`feria-ii` to `sabbatum`)
- `tagged/psalterium/prima.json`: Lent and Passiontide antiphons (`temporale`)
- `tagged/psalterium/antiphona-bmv.json`: Alma Redemptoris (`ante-nativitatem`, `post-nativitatem`)
- `tagged/psalterium/officium-capituli.json`: `oratio-sanctae-mariae`, `preces`, `oratio-dirigere`
- `tagged/psalterium/completorium.json`: `lectio`, `converte-nos`
- `tagged/psalterium/suffragia/pro-pace.json`: `antiphona`

## 8. Rendering failure (not caused by missing text)

English Matins on Easter Saturday 2027 (`/api/rite?loc=en&date=2027-04-10&occasion=matutinum-laudes+officium`) fails with
`AttributeError: 'str' object has no attribute 'get'` in `renderer/rendering_utils.py` `claw()`, called from
`try_responsory()`. It fails the same way without the imported text. The Paschal-time responsory of the Saturday Office of
Our Lady (`bmv-sabbatum`, `responsorium-i`) has only part of its English, and the `duplex-responsum` formula's English has
one part fewer than the Latin (13 against 14).

## 9. Imported scriptural texts to check against Bute

Every imported text of a kind that is usually scriptural. Divinum Officium sometimes gives Douay or its own
rendering for these instead of Bute, so each needs checking. The English shown is what was imported.

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/in-tempore-adventus.json`

  - `in-tempore-adventus, dominicae, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Blow ye the trumpet * in Zion, for the day of the Lord is nigh at hand: behold, He cometh to save us! Alleluia, Alleluia.
  - `in-tempore-adventus, feriae-iii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Drop down dew, you heavens, from above, and let the clouds rain the just: let the earth be opened and bud forth a Saviour.
  - `in-tempore-adventus, feriae-vi, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Stand ye still. And ye shall see the salvation of the Lord with you.
  - `in-tempore-adventus, dominicae, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Behold, the desire * of all nations shall come; and the house of the Lord shall be filled with glory. Alleluia.
  - `in-tempore-adventus, feriae-iii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Send forth the Lamb, O Lord, the Ruler of the land, from the rock in the wilderness unto the mount of the daughter of Zion.
  - `in-tempore-adventus, dominicae, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: The crooked * shall be made straight, and the rough places plain; O Lord, come, and make no tarrying. Alleluia.

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/quatuor-tempora-adventus.json`
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-iv, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: The Angel Gabriel was sent to a virgin espoused to a man whose name was Joseph, and the virgin's name was Mary. (Alleluia)
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-vi, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: As soon as the voice of thy salutation * sounded in mine ears, the babe leaped in my womb for joy. Alleluia.
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, sabbatum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: O thou Angel of God, * how shall this be, seeing I know not a man? Hear, O Virgin Mary! the Holy Ghost shall come upon thee, and the power of the Highest shall overshadow thee!
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-iv, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Behold the handmaid * of the Lord; be it unto me according to thy word.
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-vi, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: John bare witness of Him, * saying He That cometh after me is preferred before me.

  - `vigilia-nativitatis, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: The Saviour of the world * shall rise like the sun, and come down into the womb of the Virgin as the showers upon the grass. Alleluia.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: This day ye shall know * that the Lord cometh and in the morning, then ye shall see His glory.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: O Judah and Jerusalem, * fear not: tomorrow ye shall go out, and the Lord will be with you.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: This day ye shall know * that the Lord cometh and in the morning, then ye shall see His glory.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: On the morrow * the sins of the earth shall be washed away, and the Saviour of the world will be our King.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: The Lord cometh! * Go ye out to meet Him, and say How great is His dominion, and of His kingdom there shall be no end! He is the Mighty God, the Ruler, the Prince of Peace, Alleluia!
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: On the morrow * ye shall be saved, saith the Lord God of hosts.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/circumcisio.json`
  - `circumcisio, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: This day is set forth a wonderful mystery, * a new thing hath been created in the earth: God is made man. That which He was, He remaineth; and that which He was not, He taketh; suffering therein neither confusion nor division.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Fairer in beauty are you than the sons of men; grace is poured out upon your lips.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: In the beginning, * and before the worlds, the Word was God, and this day is He born the Saviour of the world.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: In the sun hath He set His tabernacle * and He is as a bridegroom coming out of his chamber.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: This man was born in her, * and the Highest Himself hath established her.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Lord our Saviour, * begotten before the daystar, and before the worlds, hath this day vouchsafed to be born.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Reach up * you ancient portals, that the King of glory may come in.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: All the trees of the wood shall rejoice * before the Lord, for He cometh.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: When the Lord was born the Angels sang together, * saying: Salvation to our God, Which sitteth upon the throne, and unto the Lamb.
  - `circumcisio, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: God, for His great love * wherewith He loved us, sent His Own Son in the likeness of sinful flesh. Alleluia.
  - `circumcisio, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Herein is a great mystery * of heirship; the womb of her that knew not a man is become the temple of God; in taking of her flesh, He was not defiled; all nations shall come and say: Glory be to thee, O Lord!
  - `circumcisio, antiphona, antiphona-v` — `datum`: Lo, Mary hath brought forth * the Saviour of whom when John saw him, he did proclaim: Behold the Lamb of God that taketh away the sins of the world, alleluia.
  - `circumcisio, antiphona, antiphona-v` — `in-tempore-septuagesimae`: Lo, Mary hath brought forth * the Saviour of whom when John saw him, he did proclaim: Behold the Lamb of God that taketh away the sins of the world, alleluia.
  - `circumcisio, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: O wondrous interchange! * the Creator of mankind, taking upon him a living body, vouchsafed to be born of a pure Virgin: and by his Humanity, which was begotten in no earthly wise, hath made us partakers of his Divinity.
  - `circumcisio, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: When thou wast born * all ineffably of a Virgin, then were the Scriptures fulfilled; thou camest down like the dew upon the fleece of wool, to bring salvation unto all mankind; we praise thee, O our God.
  - `circumcisio, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: In the bush which Moses saw * unconsumed, we recognize the preservation of thy glorious virginity: holy Mother of God, intercede for us.
  - `circumcisio, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: The Root of Jesse hath budded, * the Star hath come out of Jacob, the Virgin hath borne the Saviour: we praise thee, O our God.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/innocentes.json`
  - `innocentes, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: These are they which came out of great tribulation, and have washed their robes in the Blood of the Lamb.
  - `innocentes, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Innocent babies * were slain for Christ, sucklings were killed by a wicked king. Now they follow the Lamb without spot and cry without ceasing, Glory be to thee, O Lord.
  - `innocentes, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: Herod was exceeding wroth, * and slew many children in Bethlehem of Judah, the city of David.
  - `innocentes, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: Herod slew many children * from two years old, and under, for the Lord's sake.
  - `innocentes, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: Their angels * do always behold the face of the Father.
  - `innocentes, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: In Rama was there a voice * heard, weeping and mourning, Rachel weeping for her children.
  - `innocentes, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Under the throne of God * all the saints cry aloud: Avenge our blood, O our God!
  - `innocentes, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: These are they * who were not defiled with women; for they are virgins, and follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/joannes-apostolus.json`
  - `joannes-apostolus, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: A saying * went abroad among the brethren, that that disciple should not die; and Jesus did not say that he should not die, but so will I have him remain till I come.
  - `joannes-apostolus, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: Right worthy of honour * is the blessed Apostle John, who leaned on the Lord's bosom at the Last Supper.

  - `nativitas, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Glory be to God in the highest; * and on earth, peace to men of good will, alleluia, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Thine shall be the dominion * in the day of thy power, amid the brightness of the saints, from the womb, before the day-star, have I begotten thee.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: The Lord hath said unto Me: Thou art My Son, * this day have I begotten thee.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: He shall cry unto Me, alleluia. Thou art My Father, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Lord sent redemption * unto His people, He hath commanded His covenant for ever.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Lord is as a bridegroom. Coming out of his chamber.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Unto the upright there ariseth * light in the darkness; the Lord is gracious, and full of compassion, and righteous.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Grace is poured out upon your lips; thus God has blessed you forever.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: The Lord hath made known, alleluia. His salvation, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: Unto us a Christ is born, * O come, let us worship Him.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-iv` — `datum`: With the Lord * there is mercy, and with Him is plenteous redemption.
  - `nativitas, dominica-infra-octavam, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: And Jesus advanced in wisdom * and age, and grace with God and men.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: When the sun shall have risen * from heaven, ye shall see the King of kings proceeding from the Father, as a bridegroom from his chamber.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: This day the Christ is born this day the Saviour is appeared; * this day the Angels sing praise in the earth and the Archangels rejoice; this day the righteous are glad and say: Glory to God in the highest. Alleluia.
  - `nativitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-v` — `datum`: Of the fruit * of thy body will I set upon thy throne.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: The King of Peace * is magnified, whose face the whole earth desireth.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: The Peaceful King * exceeded all the kings of the whole earth.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: The days of Mary * were accomplished that she should bring forth her first-born Son.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: Know ye that the kingdom * of God is at hand? Amen, I say unto you, it will not tarry.
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Lift up your heads; * behold, your redemption draweth nigh.
  - `nativitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: O ye shepherds, speak, * and tell us what ye have seen; who is appeared in the earth? We saw the newborn Child, and angels singing praise to the Lord, alleluia, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: The Mother brought forth the King, * Whose name is called The Eternal; the joy of a Mother was hers, remaining a Virgin unsullied; neither before nor henceforth hath there been or shall be such another, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: The Angel said unto the shepherds * I bring you good tidings of great joy; for unto you is born this day a Saviour of the world, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: There was with the Angel a multitude of the heavenly host, * praising God, and saying Glory to God in the highest, and on earth peace, to men of good will, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Unto us this day a little Child is born, * and His name shall be called the Mighty God, alleluia, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, dominica-infra-octavam, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: While all things were in quiet silence * and that night was in the midst of her swift course, thine Almighty Word, O Lord, leapt down out of thy Royal Throne.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/stephanus.json`
  - `stephanus, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: He That once a little Child, Shivering in the manger lay, Set on Stephen's blessed head, A crown that fadeth not away; * O come, let us worship Him!
  - `stephanus, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Devout men buried Stephen * And made great mourning over him.
  - `stephanus, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: They stoned Stephen, * calling upon God, and saying: Lay not this sin to their charge.
  - `stephanus, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: The stones of the brook * were sweet to him; all the souls of the righteous follow him.
  - `stephanus, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: O my God, my soul followeth hard after thee, * for my flesh hath been stoned for thy sake.
  - `stephanus, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: Stephen saw the heavens opened; * he saw and entered in; blessed is he unto whom the heavens were opened.
  - `stephanus, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Behold, I see * the heavens opened, and Jesus standing on the right hand of the power of God.
  - `stephanus, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: And Stephen, full of grace * and fortitude, did great signs among the people.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/ascensio.json`
  - `ascensio, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: I ascend to my Father * and to your Father: to my God and your God, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Thy magnificence is elevated * above the heavens, O God, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Be thou exalted, O Lord, * in thy own strength: we will sing and praise, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: He is exalted exceedingly, * alleluia: above all gods, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Lord is in His holy Temple, * the Lord's throne is in heaven, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: I will extol thee, * O Lord, for thou hast upheld me, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Lord in Sion, * alleluia: is great and high, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: His going out is from the end of heaven, * and his circuit even to the end thereof, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: God is ascended * with jubilee, and the Lord with the sound of trumpet, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: The Lord in heaven, * alleluia: hath prepared his throne, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: Alleluia. The Lord Christ hath ascended up into heaven. * O come let us worship Him. Alleluia.
  - `ascensio, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Father, * I have manifested thy Name unto the men which Thou gavest Me but now I pray for them, not for the world, because I come to thee, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: O King of glory, Lord of hosts, * Who hast this day exalted thine Own Self, with great triumph, above all the heavens, leave us not orphans but send unto us the Promise of the Father, even the Spirit of truth, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: Ye men of Galilee, * why stand ye gazing up into heaven? This same Jesus, Which is taken up from you into heaven, shall so come in like manner, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: And while they looked * steadfastly towards heaven, as He went up, they said, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: He lifted up His Hands, * and blessed them, and was carried up into heaven, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: Extol the King of kings, * and ascribe praise to God, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: While they beheld, * He was taken up, and a cloud received Him in heaven, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, dominica-infra-octavam, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: But when the Comforter is come * Whom I will send unto you from the Father, He shall testify of Me, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, dominica-infra-octavam, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: These things have I told * you, that, when the time shall come, ye may remember that I told you of them, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/hebdomada-i-paschae.json`
  - `dominica-in-albis, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Now when it was late * the same day, the first of the week, and the doors were shut, where the disciples were gathered together, for fear of the Jews, Jesus came and stood in the midst and said to them: Peace be to you, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-ii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: He rising * early the first day of the week, appeared first to Mary Magdalen; out of whom he had cast seven devils, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-iii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: I go before you into Galilee, * there shall ye see Me, as I said unto you. Alleluia, Alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-iv, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: I am the True Vine. * Alleluia. And ye are My branches indeed.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-v, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Mine heart is on fire: * I would that I might see my Lord: I seek Him, and know not where they have laid Him. Alleluia, Alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-vi, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Mary Magdalene and the other Mary * came to see the Sepulchre. Alleluia.
  - `dominica-in-albis, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Now when it was late * the same day, the first of the week, and the doors were shut, where the disciples were gathered together, for fear of the Jews, Jesus came and stood in the midst and said to them: Peace be to you, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-ii, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Peace be unto you, * it is I, alleluia; fear not, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-iii, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Reach hither thine hand * and feel the print of the nails. Alleluia. And be not faithless but believing. Alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-iv, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Because thou hast seen me, * Thomas, thou hast believed: blessed are they that have not seen and have believed, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-i-paschae, feria-v, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: I have put my finger into the print of the nails, * and my hand into His Side, and I have said: My Lord, and my God! Alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/hebdomada-ii-paschae.json`
  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-ii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Go * into the whole world, alleluia: and teach all the nations, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-iii, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Going therefore * teach ye all nations; baptising them in the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-iv, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Go, tell my brethren alleluia * that they go into Galilee, alleluia, there they shall see me, alleluia, alleluia, alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-v, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Art thou only a stranger to Jerusalem * and hast not heard about Jesus, how they gave him to condemnation to death? Alleluia.
  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-vi, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Ought not Christ * to have suffered these things, and so to enter into his glory? alleluia.

  - `hebdomada-ii-paschae, feria-ii, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: The good shepherd * gives his life to his sheep, alleluia.

- `proprium-de-tempore/post-pentecosten/trinitas.json`
  - `trinitas, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Blessed be the Holy One * the Maker and Lord of all things. Blessed be the Holy and Undivided Trinity, now and always, and unto unending ages of ages.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Be present, O One Almighty God, * Father, Son, and Holy Ghost.

  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: The Father is Love; * the Son is Grace; the Holy Ghost is Communion. Blessed be the Trinity.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: We acknowledge that Thou art One in Being, * and Three in Persons.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Thou art our Hope, * Thou art our Salvation, Thou art our glory, O Blessed Trinity.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: The Father is Truth; * the Son is Truth; the Holy Ghost is Truth. Blessed be the Trinity.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: We testify * that thy Being, thy Life, and thine Understanding are One and eternally unchangeable.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Be Thou our Deliverer, * our Saviour, our Life-giver, O Blessed Trinity.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: The Being of the Father, * and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost, is one. Blessed be the Trinity.
  - `trinitas, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: The True God is Unity in Trinity, and Trinity in Unity. * O come, let us worship.
  - `trinitas, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Thanks be * to thee, O God, thanks be unto thee, O True and One Trinity, O one almighty Deity, Holy and One Unity.
  - `trinitas, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: With all our heart * and with all our voice do we acknowledge thee, praise thee, and bless thee, O God the Father the Unbegotten, O God the Only-Begotten Son, O God the Holy Ghost the Comforter, One Holy and Undivided Trinity, To thee be glory for ever.
  - `trinitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: Glory be to thee, O equal Trinity * one Deity, from before all ages, so now and for evermore.
  - `trinitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: Praise and endless glory be * to God the Father, the Son, and the Holy Paraclete, for ever and ever.
  - `trinitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: Let the glory of praise * be heard from the mouth of all to the Father, and to the begotten Son; let it equally resound with perpetual praise to the Holy Spirit.
  - `trinitas, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: Praise be to God the Father, * and to his co-equal Son, and to thee, O Holy Ghost: with perpetual delight may it resound from our lips for ever.

  - `omnes-sancti, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Be glad in the Lord, and rejoice, ye righteous, * and shout for joy, all ye that are upright in heart.

  - `omnes-sancti, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: O ye Angels, * ye Archangels, ye Thrones and Dominions, ye Principalities and Powers, ye mighty ones of the heavens, ye Cherubim and Seraphim, O ye Patriarchs and Prophets, ye holy Teachers of the Law, O ye Apostles, O all ye Martyrs of Christ, ye holy Confessors, ye Virgins of the Lord, ye Hermits, O all ye holy children of God, make intercession for us.
  - `omnes-sancti, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Oh! how glorious is the kingdom * where all the Saints rejoice with Christ; clothed in white robes, they follow the Lamb whithersoever he goeth!

  - `rosarium-bmv, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Let us this day keep * right heartily the solemn Feast of the Most Holy Rosary of Mary the Mother of God, that she may pray for us to our Lord Jesus Christ.

  - `nativitas-bmv, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Let us this day keep solemnly * the Birthday of Mary, Mother but still Maiden, her Birth, a step toward the loftiness of her throne. Alleluia.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: Let us keep the Birthday of the Virgin Mary. * Let us worship Christ, her Son, and her Lord and ours.
  - `nativitas-bmv, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Let us tell again * of the right worthy Birth of the glorious Virgin Mary, who gained the honour of Motherhood without losing the guilelessness of a Maid.
  - `nativitas-bmv, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Thy Birth, * O Virgin Mother of God, was a message of joy to the whole world, for out of thee rose the Sun of righteousness, even Christ our God. Who hath taken away the curse and brought a blessing, confounded death, and given unto us everlasting life.
  - `nativitas-bmv, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: This day was born the glorious Virgin Mary, a child of the seed of Abraham, a daughter of the tribe of Judah, a Princess of the lineage of David.
  - `nativitas-bmv, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: This day is the Birthday of the holy Virgin Mary, * whose famous life still sheddeth lustre upon all the Churches.
  - `nativitas-bmv, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: Mary was a maiden of illustrious birth, * the daughter of a Kingly race. We earnestly entreat her to help our minds and souls by her prayers.
  - `nativitas-bmv, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: With all our heart and with all our soul let us sing praise to Christ * on this the solemn Feast-day of Mary, the mighty Mother of God.
  - `nativitas-bmv, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Let us keep with rejoicing the Birthday of the Blessed Virgin Mary, * that she may pray for us to our Lord Jesus Christ.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/septem-dolores-septembris.json`
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: Come ye, and let us go * up to the mountain of the Lord, and see if there be any sorrow like unto my sorrow.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: They are increased that trouble me; * many are they that rise up against me.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: My heart is like * melting wax in the midst of my bowels.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: They bend their bow, * even bitter words, that they may shoot in secret at the perfect.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: They make ready their arrows in the quiver, * that they may privily shoot at the upright in heart.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: O God, I have declared my life * unto thee: Thou hast put my tears in thy sight.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: My soul * is sore vexed, but Thou, O Lord, how long.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: My life is spent with grief, * and my years with sighing.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: My tears have been * my meat day and night.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: God is our help * in trouble, which is come upon us exceedingly.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: Let us take our stand by the Cross, in company with Mary, the Mother of Jesus. * A sword of sorrow hath pierced through her own soul also.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, i-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Look not upon me * because I am black, because the sun hath tanned me. My mother's children were angry with me.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, ii-vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: Sorrow hath crushed me; * My face is swollen with weeping, and on mine eyelids is the shadow of death.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, antiphona` — `antiphona-i`: Whither is thy beloved gone, * O thou fairest among women? Whither is thy beloved turned aside? that we may seek him with thee.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, antiphona` — `antiphona-ii`: Look away from me. * I will weep bitterly. Labour not to comfort me.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, antiphona` — `antiphona-iii`: He hath no form nor comeliness, * and when we shall see him there is no beauty that we should desire him.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, antiphona` — `antiphona-iv`: From the sole of the foot * even unto the head, there is no soundness in it.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, antiphona` — `antiphona-v`: Revive me with flowers, * stay me up with apples for I am swooning with love.

- `ritus/officium-defunctorum.json`
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-benedictus` — `datum`: I am the resurrection and the life: * he that believeth in me, though he were dead, yet shall he live; and whosoever liveth and believeth in me shall never die.
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: The bones which thou hast broken * may rejoice.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Make my way straight before thy face, O Lord my God.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: He maketh me to lie down * in green pastures.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: Be pleased, O Lord, to deliver me * O Lord, look upon me to help me.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-i` — `datum`: I will walk before the Lord * in the land of the living.
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Lord, hear * my prayer; unto thee shall all flesh come.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Return, O Lord, deliver my soul * O save me for thy mercy's sake.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Lord, remember not the sins of my youth, * nor my transgressions.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Lord, heal my soul; * for I have sinned against thee.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: Woe is me! O Lord, * that my sojourn is long
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Thy right hand * upholdeth me.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-i, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: Lest he tear my soul like a lion, * while there is none to deliver, or to save.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-ii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: I believe that I shall yet see the goodness of the Lord * in the land of the living.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-iii, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: My soul thirsteth for the living God; * when shall I come and appear before God?
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-iii` — `datum`: The Lord shall keep thee from all evil, * the Lord shall keep thy soul.
  - `fideles-defuncti, officium-defunctorum, antiphona, antiphona-invitatorium` — `datum`: Unto the Eternal King all live. * O come, let us worship Him.
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-iv` — `datum`: From the gates of hell * deliver my soul, O Lord.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-iv` — `datum`: If Thou, Lord, shouldest mark iniquities, * O Lord, who shall stand.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-magnificat` — `datum`: All that the Father giveth Me shall come to Me; * and him that cometh to Me I will in no wise cast out.
  - `officium-defunctorum, laudes, antiphona, antiphona-v` — `datum`: Let everything that hath breath * praise the Lord.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-v` — `datum`: O Lord, forsake not * the works of thine own hands.

  - `de-passione, vesperae, antiphona, antiphona-ii` — `datum`: With them * that hated peace I was peaceable: when I spoke to them they fought against me without cause.
