  - `angeli-custodes, matutinum, nocturna-iii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** O Lord, Thou didst send thine Angel in the time of Hezekiah, King of Judah, and didst slay in the host of Sennacherib an hundred, fourscore, and five thousand. **responsum:** Wherefore now also, O Lord of heaven, send thy good Angel before us, for a fear and dread of the might of thine arm. **versus:** That those be stricken with terror that come with blasphemy against thy holy people.
- `proprium-sanctorum/october/rosarium-bmv.json`
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** Take the pleasant harp, in the time appointed, on your solemn feastday; **responsum:** And sing aloud unto the Virgin our strength. **versus:** O sing unto her a new song, declare her glory among the heathen.
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** I saw the fair one going up above the rivers of waters, a priceless savour hung heavy; **responsum:** And about her it was as the flower of roses in the spring of the year, and lilies of the valleys. **versus:** Upon thy right hand did stand the Queen in a vesture of gold, wrought about with diverse colours.
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** Thou art the glory of Jerusalem thou art the joy of Israel thou art the honour of our nation thou hast done manfully **responsum:** For thou alone hast slain all heresies. **versus:** Fair and comely art thou, terrible as a fenced camp set in battle array.
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Thy right hand is become glorious in power; thy right hand hath dashed in pieces the enemy **responsum:** They sank as lead in the mighty waters, and the sea covered them. **versus:** The Lord hath blessed thee by His power, because through thee He hath brought our enemies to nought.
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** As the vine brought I forth pleasant savour; **responsum:** And my flowers are the fruit of honour and seemliness. **versus:** I am the mother of fair love, and fear, and knowledge, and holy hope.
  - `rosarium-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Rise up, my love, and make haste. For the winter is past; the rain is over and gone **responsum:** The flowers appear on our earth. **versus:** The Lord shall give that which is good, and our land shall yield her increase.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/exaltatio-crucis.json`
  - `exaltatio-crucis, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** Lo the Church, with solemn gladness, hails the day for ever glorious, when the opening earth revealeth that dread tree of mystic triumph.
  - `exaltatio-crucis, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-iii`: **versus:** Even the Cross, whereof the glory is so excellent, and whereafter Helen, the mother of Constantine, did so diligently search until she found it.
  - `exaltatio-crucis, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** The Relique true from heaven revealed, hath now the Gospel's figure sealed
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/nativitas-bmv.json`
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** This day was the Blessed Virgin Mary born of the lineage of David. **responsum:** The same is she through whom the salvation of the world hath been manifested before the eyes of all believers. This is she whose glorious life hath given light to the world. **versus:** Let us keep with rejoicing the Birthday of the Blessed Virgin Mary.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Let us keep right heartily the Birthday of the Most Blessed Virgin Mary, **responsum:** That she may pray for us to our Lord Jesus Christ. **versus:** Let us keep with right hearty rejoicing the Birthday of the Blessed Virgin Mary.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-iii`: **incipit:** Let us tell again of the right worthy Birth of the Blessed Virgin Mary. **responsum:** The same is she whose lowliness the Lord regarded, she who by the message of an Angel conceived the Saviour of the world. **versus:** Let us keep right earnestly the Birthday of the most Blessed Virgin Mary.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** This day was born the glorious Virgin Mary, a child of the seed of Abraham, a daughter of the tribe of Judah, a Princess of the lineage of David. **responsum:** This is she whose famous life still sheddeth lustre upon all the Churches. **versus:** This day was the Blessed Virgin Mary born of the lineage of David.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Let us keep with rejoicing the Birthday of the Blessed Mary, **responsum:** That she may pray for us to our Lord Jesus Christ. **versus:** With all our heart and with all our soul let us sing praise to Christ on this the solemn Feastday of Mary the mighty Mother of God.
  - `nativitas-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-iii`: **incipit:** Thy Birth, O Virgin Mother of God, was a message of joy to the whole world; **versus:** Blessed art thou among women, and blessed is the fruit of thy womb. **responsum-i:** For out of thee rose the Sun of righteousness, even Christ our God. **responsum-ii:** Who hath taken away the curse and brought a blessing, confounded death, and given unto us everlasting life.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/septem-dolores-septembris.json`
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** There was a man whose name was Simeon; and the same man was just and devout; and he said unto Mary **responsum:** Yea, a sword shall pierce through thine own soul also. **versus:** Call me not My-pleasantness, but call me Embittered, for the Almighty hath dealt very bitterly with me.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-i, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Arise, and take the young Child and His Mother, and flee into Egypt; **responsum:** And be thou there until I bring thee word. **versus:** Out of Egypt have I called My Son, that salvation may come unto Israel.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** Jesus bearing His Cross, went forth. **responsum:** And there followed Him a company of women, which bewailed and lamented Him. **versus:** Daughters of Jerusalem, weep for yourselves and for your children.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** And when they were come to the place which is called Calvary, there they crucified Him. **responsum:** Now there stood by the Cross of Jesus His Mother. **versus:** Then was it that a sword of sorrow pierced through her blessed soul.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, responsorium, responsorium-iii`: **incipit:** Joseph of Arimathaea * Begged the Body of Jesus, and he took It down from the Cross, **versus:** The Shunamite took her dead son, and laid him on her knees; and her soul was vexed within her. **responsum-i:** He begged the Body of Jesus, and he took It down from the Cross. **responsum-ii:** And His Mother received It into her arms.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, responsorium, responsorium-i`: **incipit:** O what a sickening at heart was thine, thou Mother of sorrows **responsum:** When Joseph wrapped thy Son in linen and laid Him in the sepulchre? **versus:** Behold, and see if there be any sorrow like unto my sorrow.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, responsorium, responsorium-ii`: **incipit:** Forget not the sorrows of thy mother with thine whole heart, **responsum:** That thine offering and thy blessing may be perfected. **versus:** Hail, O maid of Royal birth, Noblest martyr-rose of earth, lily of virginity.

### Short responsories (44)

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-festo-epiphaniae/epiphania.json`
  - `epiphania, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Worship the Lord. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** In His holy courts.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/vigilia-nativitatis.json`
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** On the morrow **responsum:** Ye shall be saved. **versus:** Saith the Lord God of hosts.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** On the morrow **responsum:** the sins of the earth shall be washed away. **versus:** And the Saviour of the world will be our King.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** This day ye shall know **responsum:** That the Lord cometh. **versus:** And in the morning, then ye shall see His glory.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/nativitas.json`
  - `nativitas, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** All the ends of the earth have seen. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** The salvation of our God.
  - `nativitas, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** The Word was made flesh. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** And dwelt among us.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/ascensio.json`
  - `ascensio, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** I ascend to my Father and to your Father. **responsum:** Alleluia, alleluia. **versus:** To my God and your God.
  - `ascensio, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** When Christ ascended up on high. **responsum:** Alleluia, alleluia. **versus:** He led captivity captive.
  - `ascensio, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** God is gone up with jubilation. **responsum:** Alleluia, alleluia. **versus:** And the Lord with the sound of trumpet.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/pentecostes.json`
  - `pentecostes, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** They were all filled with the Holy Ghost. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** And began to speak.
  - `pentecostes, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** The Comforter, Which is the Holy Ghost. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** He shall teach you all things.
- `proprium-de-tempore/post-pentecosten/corpus-christi.json`
  - `corpus-christi, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Thou bringest forth food out of the earth. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** And wine that maketh glad the heart of man.
  - `corpus-christi, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** He fed them with the finest of the wheat. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** And with honey out of the rock did He satisfy them.
  - `corpus-christi, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** He gave them the bread of heaven. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** Man did eat Angels' bread.
- `proprium-de-tempore/post-pentecosten/trinitas.json`
  - `trinitas, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** By the Word of the Lord **responsum:** were the heavens made. **versus:** And all the host of them by the Breath of His mouth.
  - `trinitas, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Blessed art Thou, O Lord, **responsum:** in the firmament of heaven. **versus:** And to be praised and glorified for ever.
  - `trinitas, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Bless we the Father, and the Son, **responsum:** and the Holy Ghost. **versus:** Let us praise and exalt Him above all for ever.
- `proprium-sanctorum/aprilis/patrocinius-joseph.json`
  - `patrocinius-joseph, sexta, responsorium-breve, in-tempore-paschali`: **incipit:** I will give praise unto thy Name. **responsum:** Alleluia, Alleluia. **versus:** For Thou hast been mine Helper and Defender.
- `proprium-sanctorum/december/immaculata-conceptio-bmv.json`
  - `immaculata-conceptio-bmv, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** I will extol thee, O Lord, **responsum:** for Thou hast lifted me up. **versus:** And hast not made my foes to rejoice over me.
  - `immaculata-conceptio-bmv, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** By this I know that **responsum:** Thou favourest me. **versus:** Because mine enemy shall not triumph over me.
  - `immaculata-conceptio-bmv, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** It is Almighty God **responsum:** That girdeth me with strength. **versus:** And maketh my way perfect.
- `proprium-sanctorum/januarius/nomen-jesu.json`
  - `nomen-jesu, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** O magnify the Lord with me, **responsum:** Alleluia, alleluia. **versus:** And let us exalt His Name together.
- `proprium-sanctorum/julius/pretiosissimus-sanguis.json`
  - `pretiosissimus-sanguis, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Christ loved us, and washed us from our sins **responsum:** in His Own Blood. **versus:** And hath made us unto our God and Father a kingdom and priests.
  - `pretiosissimus-sanguis, prima, responsorium-breve, versus`: **datum:** Thou, who redeemed us with your blood.
- `proprium-sanctorum/junius/nativitas-joannis-baptistae.json`
  - `nativitas-joannis-baptistae, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Among them that are born of women **responsum:** there hath not risen a greater. **versus:** Than John the Baptist.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/apparitio-michaelis.json`
  - `apparitio-michaelis, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Before the Angels **responsum:** will I sing praise unto thee, O my God. **versus:** I will worship toward thy holy Temple, and praise thy Name.
  - `apparitio-michaelis, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** The smoke of the incense **responsum:** ascended up before the Lord. **versus:** Out of the Angel's hand.
  - `apparitio-michaelis, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** An Angel stood **responsum:** at the Altar of the Temple. **versus:** Having a golden censer in his hand.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/inventio-crucis.json`
  - `inventio-crucis, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Let all the earth worship thee, **responsum:** and sing unto thee. **versus:** Let them sing praises to thy Name, O Lord.
  - `inventio-crucis, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** We adore thee, O Christ, **responsum:** and we bless thee. **versus:** Because that through thy cross thou hast redeemed the world.
  - `inventio-crucis, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** This sign of the Cross **responsum:** shall be in heaven. **versus:** When the Lord cometh to judgment.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/joseph-sponsus.json`
  - `joseph-sponsus, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** The righteous shall grow **responsum:** as the lily. **versus:** Yea, he shall flourish for ever in the presence of the Lord.
  - `joseph-sponsus, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** His glory is great **responsum:** in thy salvation. **versus:** Honour and great majesty shalt Thou lay upon him.
  - `joseph-sponsus, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** He made him **responsum:** lord of His house. **versus:** And ruler of all His substance.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/septem-dolores-passione.json`
  - `septem-dolores-passione, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** O God, **responsum:** I have declared my life unto thee. **versus:** Thou hast put my tears in thy sight.
  - `septem-dolores-passione, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** My face is **responsum:** swollen with weeping. **versus:** And on my eyelids is the shadow (of death.)
  - `septem-dolores-passione, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** He hath made me **responsum:** desolate. **versus:** And faint with sorrow all the day.
- `proprium-sanctorum/october/rosarium-bmv.json`
  - `rosarium-bmv, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** O Holy **responsum:** Mother of God. **versus:** Thou art become beautiful and gentle in thy gladness.
  - `rosarium-bmv, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** After childbirth **responsum:** thou still remainest a Virgin. **versus:** Mother of God, pray for us.
  - `rosarium-bmv, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Holy Mother of God, **responsum:** Mary always a Virgin. **versus:** Intercede for us with the Lord our God.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/septem-dolores-septembris.json`
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, nona, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** A bundle of myrrh **responsum:** is my well-beloved unto me. **versus:** He shall lie betwixt my breasts.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, prima, responsorium-breve, versus`: **datum:** Who died for our salvation.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, sexta, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** Mine eyes **responsum:** do fail with tears. **versus:** All my bowels are troubled.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, tertia, responsorium-breve`: **incipit:** All ye **responsum:** that pass by. **versus:** Behold, and see if there be any sorrow like unto my sorrow.

### Versicles (183)

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-festo-epiphaniae/epiphania.json`
  - `epiphania, infra-octavam, commemoratio, versiculus, versiculus-alius` — `datum`: V. Worship God. Alleluia. / R. All ye His angels. Alleluia.
  - `epiphania, infra-octavam, commemoratio, versiculus` — `datum`: V. All they from Saba shall come. Alleluia. / R. They shall bring gold and incense. Alleluia.
  - `epiphania, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Let all the earth worship Thee, and sing unto Thee. / R. Let them sing praises unto Thy Name, O Lord!
  - `epiphania, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. All they from Saba shall come. Alleluia. / R. They shall bring gold and incense. Alleluia.
  - `epiphania, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Worship God. Alleluia. / R. All ye His angels. Alleluia.
  - `epiphania, vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The kings of Tarshish and of the isles shall bring presents. / R. The kings of Arabia and Saba shall offer gifts.
  - `epiphania, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. O worship the Lord, alleluia. / R. In His holy temple, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/vigilia-nativitatis.json`
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Tomorrow the iniquity of the earth shall be blotted out. / R. And the Saviour of the world shall reign over us.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, nona, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Tomorrow the iniquity of the earth shall be blotted out. / R. And the Saviour of the world shall reign over us.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. On the morrow ye shall be saved. / R. Saith the Lord God of hosts.
  - `vigilia-nativitatis, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Stand ye still. / R. And ye shall see the salvation of the Lord with you.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/circumcisio.json`
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord is as a bridegroom. / R. Coming out of his chamber.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Thou art fairer than the children of men. / R. Grace is poured into thy lips.
  - `circumcisio, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Word was made flesh. Alleluia. / R. And dwelt among us. Alleluia.
  - `circumcisio, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord hath made known, alleluia. / R. His salvation, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/innocentes.json`
  - `innocentes, ii-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. From beneath the throne of God all the saints cry out. / R. Avenge thou our blood, O our God.
  - `innocentes, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Herod being wroth, slew many children. / R. In Bethlehem of Juda, the city of David.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/joannes-apostolus.json`
  - `joannes-apostolus, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. This is that disciple who giveth testimony of these things. / R. And we know that his testimony is true.
  - `joannes-apostolus, vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Right worthy of honour is the blessed Apostle John. / R. Who leaned on the Lord's bosom at the Last Supper.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/nativitas.json`
  - `nativitas, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Tomorrow the iniquity of the earth shall be blotted out. / R. And the Saviour of the world shall reign over us.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord is as a bridegroom. / R. Coming out of his chamber.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Thou art fairer than the children of men. / R. Grace is poured into thy lips.
  - `nativitas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. He shall cry unto Me, alleluia. / R. Thou art My Father, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. All the ends of the earth have seen. Alleluia. / R. The salvation of our God. Alleluia.
  - `nativitas, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. He shall cry unto Me, alleluia. / R. Thou art My Father, alleluia.
  - `nativitas, nona, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Word was made flesh. Alleluia. / R. And dwelt among us. Alleluia.
  - `nativitas, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord hath made known, alleluia. / R. His salvation, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/stephanus.json`
  - `stephanus, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Devout men buried Stephen. / R. And made great mourning over him.
  - `stephanus, ii-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Stephen saw the heavens opened. / R. He saw and entered: blessed man, to whom the heavens were opened.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-nativitatis/vigilia-epiphaniae.json`
  - `vigilia-epiphaniae, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord hath made known, alleluia. / R. His salvation, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/ascensio.json`
  - `ascensio, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. God is ascended with jubilee, alleluia. / R. And the Lord with the sound of trumpet, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Lord in heaven, alleluia. / R. Hath prepared his throne, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. When Christ ascended up on high, alleluia. / R. He led captivity captive, alleluia.
  - `ascensio, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. I ascend to my Father and to your Father, alleluia. / R. To my God and your God, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/pentecostes.json`
  - `pentecostes, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. They were all filled with the Holy Ghost, alleluia. / R. And began to speak, alleluia.
  - `pentecostes, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Comforter, Which is the Holy Ghost, alleluia. / R. He shall teach you all things, alleluia.
  - `pentecostes, i-vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. They were all filled with the Holy Ghost, alleluia. / R. And began to speak, alleluia.
  - `pentecostes, nona, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The Apostles spoke in diverse tongues, alleluia. / R. The wonderful works of God, alleluia.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-passionis/triduum.json`
  - `coena-domini, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Mine own familiar friend, in whom I trusted; / R. Which did eat of my bread, hath lifted up his heel against me.
  - `coena-domini, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Let them be turned backward and put to confusion. / R. That desire my hurt.
  - `coena-domini, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Deliver me, O my God, out of the hand of the wicked. / R. Out of the hand of the unrighteous and cruel man.
  - `coena-domini, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Arise, O Lord. / R. Judge Thou my cause.
  - `parasceve, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. He hath set me in dark places. / R. As they that be dead of old.
  - `sabbatum-sanctum, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. My flesh shall rest in hope. / R. Neither wilt Thou suffer thine Holy One to see corruption.
  - `parasceve, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. They part my garments among them. / R. And cast lots upon my vesture.
  - `sabbatum-sanctum, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. I will both lay me down in peace. / R. And sleep.
  - `parasceve, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. False witnesses are risen up against me. / R. And iniquity hath belied itself.

  - `sabbatum-sanctum, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. His place is in peace. / R. And His dwelling-place in Zion.

  - `corpus-christi, laudes, versiculus` — `datum`: V. He maketh peace in thy borders, alleluia. / R. And filleth thee with the finest of wheat, alleluia.

  - `patrocinius-joseph, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `in-tempore-paschali`: V. I called upon the Lord, the Father of my Lord, Alleluia. / R. That He would not leave me in the days of my trouble. Alleluia.
  - `patrocinius-joseph, nona, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Planted in the house of the Lord / R. In the courts of the house of our God.

  - `patrocinius-joseph, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. The righteous shall grow as the lily. / R. Yea, he shall flourish for ever in the presence of the Lord.

  - `immaculata-conceptio-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. I will extol thee, O Lord, for Thou hast lifted me up. / R. And hast not made my foes to rejoice over me.

  - `nomen-jesu, sexta, versiculus` — `datum`: V. O magnify the Lord with me, alleluia. / R. And let us exalt His Name together, alleluia.
  - `nomen-jesu, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Give unto the Lord glory and honour, alleluia. / R. Give unto the Lord the glory due unto His Name, alleluia.

  - `septem-dolores-septembris, tertia, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Mine eyes do fail with tears. / R. All my bowels are troubled.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Pray for us, O Queen of Martyrs. / R. Who didst stand by the Cross of Jesus.
  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. A bundle of myrrh is my well-beloved unto me. / R. He shall lie betwixt my breasts.
- `ritus/officium-defunctorum.json`
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-i, versiculus` — `datum`: V. From the gates of hell. / R. Deliver their souls, O Lord.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-ii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. May the Lord set them with princes; / R. Even with the princes of his people.
  - `officium-defunctorum, matutinum, nocturna-iii, versiculus` — `datum`: V. O deliver not unto beasts the souls of them that praise thee! / R. And forget not the souls of thy poor for ever.
  - `officium-defunctorum, vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. I heard a voice from heaven, saying unto me: / R. Blessed are the dead which die in the Lord.
- `ritus/pro-sanctis.json`
  - `pro-sanctis, commemoratio, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Be glad in the Lord, and rejoice, ye righteous. / R. And shout for joy, all ye that are upright in heart.
- `votiva/de-immaculata-conceptione.json`
  - `de-immaculata-conceptione, vesperae, versiculus` — `datum`: V. Maiden Mother of God, thy stainless conception. / R. Was a message of joy to the whole world.

### Gospel passages opening homily lessons (219)

- `commune-sanctorum/doctor.json`
  - `doctor, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `datum`: At that time: Jesus said unto his disciples: Ye are the salt of the earth: But if the salt have lost his savour, wherewith shall it be salted? And so on, and that which followeth.
- `proprium-de-tempore/hebdomadae-septembris.json`
  - `quatuor-tempora-septembris, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, one of the multitude answered and said unto Jesus: Master, I have brought unto thee my son, which hath a dumb spirit. And so on.

  - `quatuor-tempora-septembris, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus spoke unto the multitudes this parable: A certain man had a fig-tree planted in his vineyard, and he came and sought fruit thereon, and found none. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-festo-epiphaniae/epiphania.json`
  - `epiphania, dies-ii, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: When Jesus therefore was born in Bethlehem of Juda, in the days of king Herod, behold, there came wise men from the east to Jerusalem. Saying, Where is he that is born king of the Jews? And so on.

  - `epiphania, dies-iv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: When Jesus therefore was born in Bethlehem of Juda, in the days of king Herod, behold, there came wise men from the east to Jerusalem. Saying, Where is he that is born king of the Jews? And so on.
  - `epiphania, dies-octava, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, John saw Jesus coming to him, and he saith: Behold the Lamb of God, behold him who taketh away the sin of the world. And so on.
  - `epiphania, dies-v, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: When Jesus therefore was born in Bethlehem of Juda, in the days of king Herod, behold, there came wise men from the east to Jerusalem. Saying, Where is he that is born king of the Jews? And so on.
  - `epiphania, dies-vi, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: When Jesus therefore was born in Bethlehem of Juda, in the days of king Herod, behold, there came wise men from the east to Jerusalem. Saying, Where is he that is born king of the Jews? And so on.
  - `epiphania, dominica-infra-octavam, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: When Jesus was twelve years old they went up to Jerusalem, after the custom of the Feast, and when they had fulfilled the days, as they returned, the child JESUS tarried behind in Jerusalem. And so on.

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-adventus/quatuor-tempora-adventus.json`
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time the angel Gabriel was sent from God into a city of Galilee, called Nazareth, To a virgin espoused to a man whose name was Joseph, of the house of David; and the virgin's name was Mary. And so on.
  - `quatuor-tempora-adventus, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: In that time, Mary, rising up, went into the hill country with haste into a city of Juda. And she entered into the house of Zachary, and saluted Elizabeth. And so on.

  - `vigilia-nativitatis, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: When as his mother Mary was espoused to Joseph, before they came together, she was found with child, of the Holy Ghost. And so on.

  - `innocentes, dies-octava, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time an angel of the Lord appeared in sleep to Joseph, saying: Arise, and take the child and his mother, and fly into Egypt: and be there until I shall tell thee. And so on.

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/hebdomada-ii-paschae.json`

- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/hebdomada-iv-paschae.json`
  - `hebdomada-iv-paschae, dominica, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said unto His disciples: I go My way to Him That sent Me, and none of you asketh Me: Whither goest Thou? And so on.

  - `hebdomada-v-paschae, dominica, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus said unto His disciples: Amen, Amen, I say unto you: Whatsoever ye shall ask the Father in My Name, He will give it you. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/pascha.json`
  - `pascha, dominica, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: And when the sabbath was past, Mary Magdalen, and Mary the mother of James, and Salome, bought sweet spices, that coming, they might anoint Jesus. And what follows.
  - `pascha, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: And behold, two of them went, the same day, to a town which was sixty furlongs from Jerusalem, named Emmaus. And so forth.
  - `pascha, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: Now whilst they were speaking these things, Jesus stood in the midst of them, and saith to them: Peace be to you; it is I, fear not. And so forth.
  - `pascha, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: After this, Jesus showed himself again to the disciples at the sea of Tiberias. And he showed himself after this manner. There were together Simon Peter, and Thomas, who is called Didymus, And so forth.
  - `pascha, feria-v, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: But Mary stood at the sepulchre without, weeping. Now as she was weeping, she stooped down, and looked into the sepulchre. And so forth.
  - `pascha, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: And the eleven disciples went into Galilee, unto the mountain where Jesus had appointed them. And so forth.
  - `pascha, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: And on the first day of the week, Mary Magdalen cometh early, when it was yet dark, unto the sepulchre. And so forth.

  - `pentecostes, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said unto Nicodemus: God so loved the world that He gave His Only-begotten Son, that whosoever believeth in Him should not perish, but have everlasting life. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: Jesus said unto the Pharisees: Amen, amen, I say unto you, he that entereth not by the door into the sheep-fold, but climbeth up some other way, the same is a thief and a robber but he that entereth by the door is the shepherd of the sheep. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said unto the multitudes of the Jews: No man can come to Me, except the Father, Which hath sent me, draw him. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, feria-v, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: Jesus called His twelve disciples together, and gave them power and authority over all devils, and to cure diseases. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, it came to pass on a certain day, as Jesus sat and taught, that there were Pharisees, and Doctors of the law sitting by, which were come out of every town in Galilee, and Judaea and Jerusalem and the power of the Lord was present to heal them. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, festum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said unto His disciples: If a man love Me, He will keep My word, and My Father will love him, and We will come unto him, and make Our abode with him. And so on.
  - `pentecostes, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time Jesus arose out of the synagogue, and entered into Simon's house. And Simon's wife's mother was taken with a great fever. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/rogatio.json`
  - `feria-ii-rogationis, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus said unto His disciples Which of you shall have a friend, and shall go unto him at midnight, and say unto him Friend, lend me three loaves. And so on.
  - `feria-iv-rogationis, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus lifted up His Eyes to heaven, and spoke these words: Father, the hour is come; glorify thy Son. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-paschali/vigilia-pentecostes.json`
  - `vigilia-pentecostes, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus said unto His disciples: If ye love Me, keep My commandments. And I will pray the Father, and He shall give you another Comforter. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-passionis/hebdomada-passionis.json`
  - `hebdomada-passionis, dominica, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said to the multitude of Jews: Which of you shall convince me of sin? If I say the truth to you, why do you not believe me? And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, the rulers and Pharisees sent ministers to apprehend him. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time: Jesus walked in Galilee; for he would not walk in Judea, because the Jews sought to kill him. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, it was the feast of the dedication at Jerusalem: and it was winter. And Jesus walked in the temple, in Solomon's porch. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, feria-v, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, one of the Pharisees desired him to eat with him. And he went into the house of the Pharisee, And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, chief priests and the Pharisees gathered a council, and said: What do we, for this man doth many miracles? If we let him alone so, all will believe in him; and the Romans will come, and take away our place and nation. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-passionis, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, the chief priests thought to kill Lazarus also: Because many of the Jews, by reason of him, went away, and believed in Jesus. And so on.

  - `hebdomada-sancta, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: Six days before the pasch, Jesus came to Bethania, where Lazarus had been dead, whom Jesus raised to life.

And all nations shall be gathered together before him. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-i-quadragesimae, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, when Jesus was come into Jerusalem, the whole city was moved, saying: Who is this? And so on.
  - `hebdomada-i-quadragesimae, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, some of the scribes and Pharisees answered him, saying: Master, we would see a sign from thee. Who answering said to them: An evil and adulterous generation seeketh a sign. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-i-quadragesimae, feria-v, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus went from thence, and retired into the coasts of Tyre and Sidon. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-i-quadragesimae, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time was a festival day of the Jews, and Jesus went up to Jerusalem. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-i-quadragesimae, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus taketh unto him Peter and James, and John his brother, and bringeth them up into a high mountain apart: And he was transfigured before them. And so on.
- `proprium-de-tempore/in-tempore-quadragesimae/hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae.json`
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, dominica, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus taketh unto him Peter and James, and John his brother, and bringeth them up into a high mountain apart: And he was transfigured before them. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: In that time, Jesus said to the multitude of Jews: I go, and you shall seek me, and you shall die in your sin. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus spoke to the multitudes and to his disciples, saying: The scribes and the Pharisees have sitten on the chair of Moses. All things therefore whatsoever they shall say to you, observe and do: but according to their works do ye not; for they say, and do not. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus, going up to Jerusalem, took the twelve disciples apart, and said to them: Behold we go up to Jerusalem, and the Son of man shall be betrayed to the chief priests and the scribes, and they shall condemn him to death. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, feria-v, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus said to the pharisees: There was a certain rich man, who was clothed in purple and fine linen; and feasted sumptuously every day. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus said to the multitude of Jews and the chief priests: Hear ye another parable. There was a man an householder, who planted a vineyard, and made a hedge round about it. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-ii-quadragesimae, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus said to the pharisees and scribes in parable: A certain man had two sons: And the younger of them said to his father: Father, give me the portion of substance that falleth to me. And so on.

  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, feria-ii, matutinum, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus said to the pharisees: Doubtless you will say to me this similitude: Physician, heal thyself: as great things as we have heard done in Capharnaum, do also here in thy own country. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, feria-iii, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus saith to his disciples: But if thy brother shall offend against thee, go, and rebuke him between thee and him alone. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, feria-iv, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, scribes and Pharisees came to Jesus from Jerusalem, saying: Why do thy disciples trangress the tradition of the ancients? And so on.
  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, feria-v, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus arose out of the synagogue, and entered into Simon's house. And Simon's wife's mother was taken with a great fever. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, feria-vi, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus cometh therefore to a city of Samaria, which is called Sichar, near the land which Jacob gave to his son Joseph. And so on.
  - `hebdomada-iii-quadragesimae, sabbatum, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus went unto Mount Olivet. And early in the morning he came again into the temple. And so on.

  - `nativitas-joannis-baptistae, dies-iv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: Elizabeth's full time came that she should be delivered and she brought forth a son. And her neighbours and her cousins heard how the Lord had showed great mercy upon her and they rejoiced with her. And so on
  - `nativitas-joannis-baptistae, dies-octava, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: Elizabeth's full time came that she should be delivered and she brought forth a son. And her neighbours and her cousins heard how the Lord had showed great mercy upon her and they rejoiced with her. And so on.
  - `nativitas-joannis-baptistae, duplex-i-classis, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: Elisabeth's fullness of time came that she should deliver, and she brought forth a son. And her neighbours and her cousins heard how the Lord had showed great mercy upon her, and they rejoiced with her. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/junius/petrus-paulus.json`
  - `petrus-paulus, dies-octava, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus constrained His disciples to get into a ship, and to go before Him unto the other side, while He sent the multitudes away. And so on.
  - `petrus-paulus, duplex-i-classis, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time: Jesus came into the coasts of Caesarea Philippi, and He asked His disciples, saying: Who do men say that I, the Son of man, am? And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/junius/vigilia-nativitatis-joannis-baptistae.json`
  - `vigilia-nativitatis-joannis-baptistae, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: There was, in the days of Herod the King of Judaea, a certain Priest named Zacharias, of the course of Abia; and his wife was of the daughters of Aaron, and her name was Elizabeth. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/junius/vigilia-petri-pauli.json`
  - `vigilia-petri-pauli, matutinum, lectio, lectio-commemorationis` — `datum`: At that time: Jesus saith to Simon Peter, Simon, son of Jonas, lovest thou me more than these? And so on, and that which followeth.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/apparitio-michaelis.json`
  - `apparitio-michaelis, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, the disciples came to Jesus, saying: Who thinkest thou is the greater in the kingdom of heaven? And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/cor-jesus.json`
  - `cor-jesus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time The Jews, because it was the Preparation, that the bodies should not remain upon the cross on the Sabbath-day (for that Sabbath was an high day) besought Pilate that their legs might be broken, and that they might be taken away. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/inventio-crucis.json`
  - `inventio-crucis, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time There was a man of the Pharisees named Nicodemus, a ruler of the Jews. The same came to Jesus by night, and said unto Him Rabbi, we know that Thou art a Teacher come from God. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/joannes-portam-latinam.json`
  - `joannes-portam-latinam, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: AT that time: Came to Jesus the mother of Zebedee's children, with her sons, worshipping Him, and desiring a certain thing of Him. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/monica.json`
  - `monica, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus went into a city called Naim; and His disciples went with Him, and much people. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/nereus-achilleus-domitilla-pancratius.json`
  - `nereus-achilleus-domitilla-pancratius, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time There was a certain nobleman, whose son was sick at Capernaum. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/petrus-coelestinus.json`
  - `petrus-coelestinus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Peter said to Jesus said to him: Behold we have left all things, and have followed thee: what therefore shall we have? And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/majus/philippus-jacobus.json`
  - `philippus-jacobus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus said unto His disciples: "Let not your heart be troubled. Ye believe in God, believe also in Me. In My Father’s house there are many mansions." And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/annuntiatio-bmv.json`
  - `annuntiatio-bmv, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time the angel Gabriel was sent from God into a city of Galilee, called Nazareth, To a virgin espoused to a man whose name was Joseph, of the house of David; and the virgin's name was Mary. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/cyrillus-hierosolymitani.json`
  - `cyrillus-hierosolymitani, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus said to his disciples: And when they shall persecute you in this city, flee into another. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/joannes-deo.json`
  - `joannes-deo, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time The Pharisees came together: And one of them, a doctor of the law, asking him, tempting him: Master, which is the greatest commandment in the law? And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/joseph-sponsus.json`
  - `joseph-sponsus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: When as his mother Mary was espoused to Joseph, before they came together, she was found with child, of the Holy Ghost. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/martius/septem-dolores-passione.json`
  - `septem-dolores-passione, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: In that time stood by the cross of Jesus, his mother, and his mother's sister, Mary of Cleophas, and Mary Magdalen. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/november/andreas.json`
  - `andreas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio, lectio-i` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus, walking by the sea of Galilee, saw two brethren, Simon who is called Peter, and Andrew his brother, casting a net into the sea (for they were fishers). And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/november/caecilia.json`
  - `caecilia, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time, Jesus said to his disciples: Then shall the kingdom of heaven be like to ten virgins, who taking their lamps went out to meet the bridegroom and the bride. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/november/gregorius-thaumaturgus.json`
  - `gregorius-thaumaturgus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: And Jesus answering, saith to them: Have the faith of God. Amen I say to you, that whosoever shall say to this mountain, Be thou removed and be cast into the sea, and shall not stagger in his heart, but believe, that whatsoever he saith shall be done; it shall be done unto him. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/november/martinus.json`
  - `martinus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time: Jesus said to his disciples: No man lighteth a candle, and putteth it in a hidden place, nor under a bushel; but upon a candlestick, that they that come in, may see the light. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/november/omnes-sancti.json`
  - `omnes-sancti, dies-ii, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time: Jesus, seeing the multitudes, he went up into a mountain, and when he was set down, his disciples came unto him. And so on.
  - `omnes-sancti, dies-iii, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus, seeing the multitudes, went up into a mountain, and, when He was set, His disciples came unto Him. And so on.

  - `simon-et-judas, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: at that time, Jesus said to his disciples: These things I command you, that you love one another. If the world hate you, know ye, that it hath hated me before you. And so on.

  - `vigilia-simonis-et-judae, matutinum, lectio` — `lectio-i`: In that time Jesus said to his disciples: I an the true vine; and my Father is the husbandman. And so on.

  - `exaltatio-crucis, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus said unto the multitudes of the Jews: Now is the judgment of this world, now shall the prince of this world be cast out. And so on.

  - `hieronymus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus said unto His disciples: Ye are the salt of the earth. But if the salt have lost his savour, wherewith shall it be salted? And so on.

  - `josephus-cupertino, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time Jesus spake unto the chief priests and Pharisees by parables, and said The kingdom of heaven is like unto a certain king, which made a marriage for his son. And so on.

  - `matthaeus, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus saw a man, named Matthew, sitting at the receipt of custom; and He saith unto him Follow Me. And so on.

  - `nativitas-bmv, dies-v, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: The book of the generation of Jesus Christ, the Son of David, the son of Abraham. Abraham begat Isaac, and Isaac begat Jacob. And so on.

  - `septem-dolores-septembris, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time: There stood by the Cross of Jesus His Mother, and His Mother's sister, Mary (the wife) of Cleophas, and Mary Magdalene. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/stephanus-hungariae.json`
  - `stephanus-hungariae, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time, Jesus spoke this parable unto His disciples: A certain nobleman went into a far country to receive for himself a kingdom and to return. And so on.
- `proprium-sanctorum/september/vigilia-matthaei.json`
  - `vigilia-matthaei, matutinum, lectio, lectio-commemorationis` — `datum`: At that time, Jesus saw a publican, named Levi, sitting at the receipt of custom, and He said unto him: Follow me. And so on.
- `votiva/de-joseph.json`
  - `de-joseph, matutinum, nocturna-iii, lectio` — `lectio-i`: At that time: When all the people were baptized, it came to pass, that Jesus also being baptized and praying, the heaven was opened. And so on, and that which followeth.
